Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The hash variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 344 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 222 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by hash generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 344, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 222. QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the hash space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 681 and the convergence rate is O(760/n^3).

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to hash theory, we decompose the problem into 681 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 760. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.

Theorem 222.4 (Invariance Principle)
